{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01ScXEyZLYfFEZbSghrRc2F1","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tname: aria-acceptance-lead\n3\truntime_profile: planner_orchestrator\n4\tdescription: Project lead for the ARIA acceptance lane. Plans an acceptance run, dispatches the output-validator + gap-hunter via the Agent tool, reads the DETERMINISTIC harness results (tools/aria-acceptance/harness.py), and decides accept/reject + sequences gap closure. You\n28\tdo not produce verdicts yourself — you run the deterministic harness, dispatch\n29\tspecialists for judgment, and decide what ships.\n30\t\n31\t## The iron rule\n32\t\n33\t**The deterministic harness is the only source of truth; agent verdicts are leads.**\n34\t\n35\t**Why:** ARIA's whole value is \"trust evidence, not assertions\". An LLM watcher\n36\tthat judged ARIA by reading logs would reintroduce exactly the hallucination ARIA\n37\twas built to reject — so the accept/reject gate must be a deterministic assertion\n38\t(`tools/aria-acceptance/harness.py`), and an agent's opinion is only an annotation\n39\ton top of it.\n40\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"15e2d44a-5660-4a51-a46d-eca1518eadb6","uuid":"485be2b9-9eae-4726-94e3-62206099dd2d","timestamp":"2026-09-25T10:46:03.096Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-consensus-arbiter-00bb0e8ea72e/.claude/agents/aria-acceptance-lead.md","content":"---\nname: aria-acceptance-lead\nruntime_profile: planner_orchestrator\ndescription: Project lead for the ARIA acceptance lane. Plans an acceptance run, dispatches the output-validator + gap-hunter via the Agent tool, reads the DETERMINISTIC harness results (tools/aria-acceptance/harness.py), and decides accept/reject + sequences gap closure. Distinct from ARIA's own runtime agents — this lane audits ARIA from the outside.\nmodel: opus\neffort: max\ntools: Read, Grep, Glob, Agent\npedagogy-tier: 2\ndispatch: ad-hoc\n---\n\n# ARIA Acceptance Lead — external acceptance & gap-closure coordinator\n\n## Canonical References (READ via the Read tool before starting)\n\n- @docs/aria/ARIA-NASIL-CALISIR.md\n- @.claude/knowledge/layer-1-aria.md\n- @.claude/knowledge/layer-2-aria-canonical-envelope.md\n- @docs/aria/SPEC.md\n- @docs/aria/CONTRACTS.md\n- @docs/aria/PIPELINES.md\n- @docs/aria/CURRENT_STATE.md\n- @tools/aria-acceptance/harness.py\n\n## What you own\n\nYou coordinate the acceptance of ARIA's outputs and the closing of its gaps. You\ndo not produce verdicts yourself — you run the deterministic harness, dispatch\nspecialists for judgment, and decide what ships.\n\n## The iron rule\n\n**The deterministic harness is the only source of truth; agent verdicts are leads.**\n\n**Why:** ARIA's whole value is \"trust evidence, not assertions\". An LLM watcher\nthat judged ARIA by reading logs would reintroduce exactly the hallucination ARIA\nwas built to reject — so the accept/reject gate must be a deterministic assertion\n(`tools/aria-acceptance/harness.py`), and an agent's opinion is only an annotation\non top of it.\n","numLines":40,"startLine":1,"totalLines":92}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VCFydnWnxTtc5GujZr5UVs","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Per-agent runtime profile (model + reasoning effort) SSoT reader.\n2\t\n3\tPlan 023 §A — model/effort tiering. Until this module existed every ARIA\n4\tagent ran on the single most expensive setting and the frontmatter\n5\t``model:``/``effort:`` fields were declared but never consumed at runtime.\n6\t\n7\tThe fix follows the \"scout-and-verify\" operator decision: the cheap tier\n8\t(read-only scorers / scanners) flags candidates, the expensive tier\n9\t(planners / arbiter / writers) decides and re-verifies and never trusts the\n10\tcheap tier blindly. The agent frontmatter is the single source of truth for\n11\twhich tier each agent runs on. Two backends consume it:\n12\t\n13\t* Claude Code Agent dispatch honours ``model:`` natively.\n14\t* The Claude Code CLI executor (``tools/aria-poc/claude_runtime.py``) resolves\n15\t  ``model:`` to the ``--model`` alias via :func:`resolve_claude_model`.\n16\t\n17\tThis module is the only reader, so both consumers can never drift from the\n18\tfrontmatter. Fail-safe by design: an unknown agent or a missing/invalid field\n19\tresolves to the strongest SELECTED tier (``opus`` / ``max``). A silent cost\n20\tdowngrade can therefore never be introduced by omission — only by an explicit,\n21\treviewable frontmatter edit. Operator decision 2026-09-12: ``fable`` stays a\n22\tnamed tier (ordering, pricing) and is selected by nothing — no profile, no\n23\tfrontmatter, no default; ``tests/invariants`` pins it. The same decision\n24\tmade every tier a credit LEAF: an exhausted provider is requeued under its\n25\tcooldown (``provider_cooldown``), never retried on a weaker tier, and the\n26\tonly ladder left (``claude_runtime.AUTH_FAILOVER_TIER``) serves AUTH\n27\tfailures across vendors.\n28\t\"\"\"\n29\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n30\t\n31\timport re\n32\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n33\tfrom functools import lru_cache\n34\tfrom pathlib import Path\n35\t\n36\t\n37\t# ORPHAN-HIGH-763 — `glm-5.3` (Z.ai) joins the dispatchable vocabulary.\n38\t# It reaches the same `claude` binary through the provider swap Z.ai\n39\t# publishes (ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=https://api.z.ai/api/anthropic), so this is\n40\t# one more member of an existing vocabulary rather than a second runtime.\n41\t# The lane still refuses API-key mode unless ARIA_ALLOW_CLAUDE_API_KEY_MODE=1\n42\t# is set \"under a new policy\" (claude_runtime.assert_claude_policy_environment)\n43\t# — that operator policy, not this constant, is what actually enables it.\n44\tVALID_MODELS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\"opus\", \"sonnet\", \"haiku\", \"fable\", \"glm-5.3\"})\n45\tVALID_EFFORTS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\"low\", \"medium\", \"high\", \"xhigh\", \"max\"})\n46\t\n47\tDEFAULT_MODEL: str = \"opus\"\n48\t\n49\t# E16 (ORPHAN-673) — model-tier write protection SSoT (operator rule\n50\t# 2026-08-13): a weaker model must never delete or overwrite what a\n51\t# stronger model authored, and models below the authoring floor must not\n52\t# author agents at all. Strongest first; future stronger models are\n53\t# PREPENDED here deliberately — never inferred.\n54\t#\n55\t# `glm-5.3` sits between fable and opus by OPERATOR DECISION (2026-08-20).\n56\t# The consequence is deliberate and stated here because the ordering IS the\n57\t# authority: ranking above `MIN_AGENT_AUTHORING_TIER` lets it author agents,\n58\t# and opus may not overwrite what it authored. Note what admission COSTS: an\n59\t# unlisted model resolves asymmetrically (weakest as actor, strongest as\n60\t# target), so before this line glm output was protected from everyone and\n61\t# could author nothing. Listing it trades that blanket protection for a\n62\t# stated rank — which is the honest position once a model actually runs.\n63\tMODEL_TIER_ORDER: tuple[str, ...] = (\"fable\", \"glm-5.3\", \"opus\", \"sonnet\", \"haiku\")\n64\tMIN_AGENT_AUTHORING_TIER: str = \"opus\"\n65\t\n66\t\n67\tdef model_tier_rank(model: str | None) -> int:\n68\t    \"\"\"Rank in MODEL_TIER_ORDER; lower is stronger.\n69\t\n70\t    Unknown models resolve ASYMMETRICALLY by design: as a TARGET's author\n71\t    they rank strongest (-1) — an agent stamped by a model this kernel\n72\t    does not know is most plausibly a FUTURE, stronger model and must be\n73\t    protected; as an ACTIVE actor they rank weakest (len) — a model that\n74\t    cannot prove its tier may neither author nor modify. Callers pick the\n75\t    side via the dedicated asserts below.\n76\t    \"\"\"\n77\t    if model in MODEL_TIER_ORDER:\n78\t        return MODEL_TIER_ORDER.index(model)\n79\t    return -1\n80\t\n81\t\n82\tdef _active_model_rank(model: str | None) -> int:\n83\t    if model in MODEL_TIER_ORDER:\n84\t        return MODEL_TIER_ORDER.index(model)\n85\t    return len(MODEL_TIER_ORDER)\n86\t\n87\t\n88\tdef assert_model_may_author_agents(model: str | None) -> None:\n89\t    \"\"\"E16 authoring floor: below MIN_AGENT_AUTHORING_TIER cannot author.\"\"\"\n90\t    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError\n91\t\n92\t    if _active_model_rank(model) > MODEL_TIER_ORDER.index(MIN_AGENT_AUTHORING_TIER):\n93\t        raise GovernanceError(\n94\t            f\"agent_authoring_tier_too_low: model={model!r} \"\n95\t            f\"floor={MIN_AGENT_AUTHORING_TIER!r} order={MODEL_TIER_ORDER!r}\"\n96\t        )\n97\t\n98\t\n99\tdef assert_model_may_modify_agent(\n100\t    *, active_model: str | None, target_authored_by: str | None\n101\t) -> None:\n102\t    \"\"\"E16 write protection: weaker may not overwrite stronger's work.\n103\t\n104\t    An UNSTAMPED target (legacy agents predating authored_by_model) is\n105\t    modifiable by any authoring-eligible model — the rule protects\n106\t    provenance that exists, it does not invent provenance. The duel has\n107\t    NO exception: a lower-tier duel winner still cannot supersede a\n108\t    higher-tier agent directly; that path must fall to HUMAN_REQUIRED.\n109\t    \"\"\"\n110\t    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError\n111\t\n112\t    if not target_authored_by:\n113\t        return\n114\t    if _active_model_rank(active_model) > model_tier_rank(target_authored_by):\n115\t        raise GovernanceError(\n116\t            \"model_tier_insufficient_to_modify: \"\n117\t            f\"active={active_model!r} target_authored_by={target_authored_by!r} \"\n118\t            f\"order={MODEL_TIER_ORDER!r} (duel has no exception — escalate \"\n119\t            \"to HUMAN_REQUIRED)\"\n120\t        )\n121\t# ORPHAN-HIGH-477 — ultracode depth. `max` is the CLI's deepest reasoning\n122\t# level (verified against Claude Code 2.1.220: --effort low|medium|high|xhigh|max).\n123\t# The fail-safe deliberately resolves UPWARD, so an unknown agent or an\n124\t# unparseable frontmatter field can never quietly run shallower than policy.\n125\tDEFAULT_EFFORT: str = \"max\"\n126\t\n127\t# Agents that hold write tools (Edit/Write/Bash) or author governance\n128\t# artifacts MUST stay on the expensive tier — the cheap scout tier is for\n129\t# read-only judgment only. Enforced by the model-tier invariant test so a\n130\t# frontmatter edit can never quietly downgrade a writer.\n131\tWRITE_TIER_AGENTS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\n132\t    \"aria-implementer\",\n133\t    \"aria-drafter\",\n134\t    \"aria-prompt-writer\",\n135\t    # Plan 030 / K3 — write-capable agents the jest mirror already pinned;\n136\t    # the two sets must never diverge (ORPHAN-HIGH-285).\n137\t    \"aria-acceptance-gap-fixer\",\n138\t    \"aria-worker\",\n139\t})\n140\t\n141\t_FRONTMATTER_RX = re.compile(r\"\\A---\\n(.*?)\\n---\", re.DOTALL)\n142\t\n143\t\n144\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n145\tclass AgentRuntimeProfile:\n146\t    \"\"\"Resolved envelope for one agent. ``source`` records how it was derived.\n147\t\n148\t    Plan 032 Faz 032b — the envelope is more than a tier. When the agent's\n149\t    frontmatter names a kernel-owned ``runtime_profile``, model/effort/tools/\n150\t    write_scope/env_passthrough/external_writes come from\n151\t    ``runtime_profiles.json`` (the authority the agent cannot edit) and the\n152\t    frontmatter is only a mirror. ``source`` then reads ``kernel_profile``;\n153\t    a mirror that disagrees with the kernel is recorded as\n154\t    ``kernel_profile_mirror_drift`` and the KERNEL value is what runs.\n155\t    Legacy agents without the key keep the frontmatter-only resolution.\n156\t    \"\"\"\n157\t\n158\t    agent_name: str\n159\t    model: str\n160\t    effort: str\n161\t    source: str  # \"kernel_profile\" | \"kernel_profile_mirror_drift\" | \"frontmatter\" | \"default_missing_file\" | \"default_invalid\"\n162\t    profile_id: str | None = None\n163\t    tools: tuple[str, ...] = ()\n164\t    write_scope: tuple[str, ...] = ()\n165\t    env_passthrough: tuple[str, ...] = ()\n166\t    external_writes: bool = False\n167\t    budget_usd_per_run: float | None = None\n168\t    max_concurrent: int | None = None\n169\t    runtime: str = \"claude\"\n170\t\n171\t    @property\n172\t    def write_capable(self) -> bool:\n173\t        from .runtime_profiles import WRITE_TOOLS\n174\t\n175\t        return bool(WRITE_TOOLS & set(self.tools))\n176\t\n177\t\n178\tdef _repo_root() -> Path:\n179\t    # <repo>/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/agent_runtime_profile.py\n180\t    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\n181\t\n182\t\n183\tdef _agents_dir(repo_root: Path | None) -> Path:\n184\t    return (repo_root or _repo_root()) / \".claude\" / \"agents\"\n185\t\n186\t\n187\tdef _find_agent_file(agent_name: str, repo_root: Path | None) -> Path | None:\n188\t    base = _agents_dir(repo_root)\n189\t    if not base.exists():\n190\t        return None\n191\t    direct = base / f\"{agent_name}.md\"\n192\t    if direct.is_file():\n193\t        return direct\n194\t    matches = sorted(base.glob(f\"**/{agent_name}.md\"))\n195\t    return matches[0] if matches else None\n196\t\n197\t\n198\tdef _parse_frontmatter_field(text: str, field: str) -> str | None:\n199\t    match = _FRONTMATTER_RX.match(text)\n200\t    if not match:\n201\t        return None\n202\t    fm = match.group(1)\n203\t    field_match = re.search(rf\"^{re.escape(field)}:\\s*(\\S+)\\s*$\", fm, re.MULTILINE)\n204\t    if not field_match:\n205\t        return None\n206\t    return field_match.group(1).strip().strip(\"\\\"'\").lower()\n207\t\n208\t\n209\t@lru_cache(maxsize=256)\n210\tdef _read_profile_cached(agent_name: str, repo_root_str: str | None) -> AgentRuntimeProfile:\n211\t    repo_root = Path(repo_root_str) if repo_root_str else None\n212\t    path = _find_agent_file(agent_name, repo_root)\n213\t    if path is None:\n214\t        return AgentRuntimeProfile(agent_name, DEFAULT_MODEL, DEFAULT_EFFORT, \"default_missing_file\")\n215\t    text = path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n216\t    raw_model = _parse_frontmatter_field(text, \"model\")\n217\t    raw_effort = _parse_frontmatter_field(text, \"effort\")\n218\t    # Plan 032 Faz 032b — a kernel-owned profile is the authority when named.\n219\t    from .runtime_profiles import RUNTIME_PROFILE_FRONTMATTER_KEY, load_runtime_profiles\n220\t\n221\t    profile_id = _parse_frontmatter_field(text, RUNTIME_PROFILE_FRONTMATTER_KEY)\n222\t    if profile_id:\n223\t        kernel = load_runtime_profiles().get(profile_id)\n224\t        if kernel is None:\n225\t            # Naming an unknown profile is an invalid frontmatter, and the\n226\t            # fail-safe is the same as any other invalid field: the most\n227\t            # expensive read-only tier, with no envelope widening.\n228\t            return AgentRuntimeProfile(agent_name, DEFAULT_MODEL, DEFAULT_EFFORT, \"default_invalid\")\n229\t        source = \"kernel_profile\"\n230\t        if raw_model != kernel.model or raw_effort != kernel.effort:\n231\t            source = \"kernel_profile_mirror_drift\"\n232\t        return AgentRuntimeProfile(\n233\t            agent_name, kernel.model, kernel.effort, source,\n234\t            profile_id=kernel.profile_id,\n235\t            tools=kernel.tools,\n236\t            write_scope=kernel.write_scope,\n237\t            env_passthrough=kernel.env_passthrough,\n238\t            external_writes=kernel.external_writes,\n239\t            budget_usd_per_run=kernel.budget_usd_per_run,\n240\t            max_concurrent=kernel.max_concurrent,\n241\t            runtime=kernel.runtime,\n242\t        )\n243\t    model = raw_model if raw_model in VALID_MODELS else DEFAULT_MODEL\n244\t    effort = raw_effort if raw_effort in VALID_EFFORTS else DEFAULT_EFFORT\n245\t    source = \"frontmatter\"\n246\t    if raw_model not in VALID_MODELS or raw_effort not in VALID_EFFORTS:\n247\t        source = \"default_invalid\"\n248\t    return AgentRuntimeProfile(agent_name, model, effort, source)\n249\t\n250\t\n251\tdef read_agent_runtime_profile(\n252\t    agent_name: str,\n253\t    *,\n254\t    repo_root: str | Path | None = None,\n255\t) -> AgentRuntimeProfile:\n256\t    \"\"\"Resolve the (model, effort) tier declared in an agent's frontmatter.\n257\t\n258\t    Fail-safe: unknown agent or invalid/missing field → most expensive tier.\n259\t    \"\"\"\n260\t    if not isinstance(agent_name, str) or not agent_name.strip():\n261\t        return AgentRuntimeProfile(str(agent_name), DEFAULT_MODEL, DEFAULT_EFFORT, \"default_invalid\")\n262\t    root_str = str(Path(repo_root).resolve()) if repo_root is not None else None\n263\t    return _read_profile_cached(agent_name.strip(), root_str)\n264\t\n265\t\n266\tdef resolve_claude_model(\n267\t    agent_name: str,\n268\t    *,\n269\t    repo_root: str | Path | None = None,\n270\t) -> str:\n271\t    \"\"\"Claude Code CLI executor lever: resolve an agent's frontmatter\n272\t    ``model`` tier to the ``--model`` alias the CLI consumes. Fail-safe to\n273\t    the most expensive tier for unknown agents or invalid fields.\"\"\"\n274\t    return read_agent_runtime_profile(agent_name, repo_root=repo_root).model\n275\t\n276\t\n277\tdef resolve_claude_effort(\n278\t    agent_name: str,\n279\t    *,\n280\t    repo_root: str | Path | None = None,\n281\t) -> str:\n282\t    \"\"\"Claude Code CLI executor lever: resolve an agent's frontmatter\n283\t    ``effort`` tier to the ``--effort`` level the CLI consumes. Shares the\n284\t    fail-safe path with :func:`resolve_claude_model` (most expensive tier).\"\"\"\n285\t    return read_agent_runtime_profile(agent_name, repo_root=repo_root).effort\n286\t\n287\t# E16 — the provenance stamp lives WITH the tier order: stamping is a\n288\t# governance concern of the runtime-profile SSoT, not draft rendering\n289\t# (agent_genesis is barred from markdown/frontmatter literals by\n290\t# I-V3-12c, and rightly so — the kernel governs provenance, the drafter\n291\t# renders content).\n292\tdef parse_authored_by_model(text: str) -> str | None:\n293\t    match = re.search(\n294\t        r\"^authored_by_model:\\s*([A-Za-z0-9._-]+)\\s*$\", text, flags=re.MULTILINE\n295\t    )\n296\t    return match.group(1) if match else None\n297\t\n298\t\n299\tdef stamp_authored_by_model(body: str, model: str) -> str:\n300\t    \"\"\"Kernel-injected provenance stamp (E16). A drafter-supplied value\n301\t    is OVERWRITTEN — the stamp is measured at mint, never claimed.\"\"\"\n302\t    if parse_authored_by_model(body) is not None:\n303\t        return re.sub(\n304\t            r\"^authored_by_model:\\s*[A-Za-z0-9._-]+\\s*$\",\n305\t            f\"authored_by_model: {model}\",\n306\t            body,\n307\t            count=1,\n308\t            flags=re.MULTILINE,\n309\t        )\n310\t    if body.startswith(\"---\\n\"):\n311\t        return body.replace(\"---\\n\", f\"---\\nauthored_by_model: {model}\\n\", 1)\n312\t    return f\"---\\nauthored_by_model: {model}\\n---\\n{body}\"\n313\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"15e2d44a-5660-4a51-a46d-eca1518eadb6","uuid":"7b4c4d91-33d9-4596-9330-b6177070dc82","timestamp":"2026-09-25T10:46:15.722Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-consensus-arbiter-00bb0e8ea72e/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/agent_runtime_profile.py","content":"\"\"\"Per-agent runtime profile (model + reasoning effort) SSoT reader.\n\nPlan 023 §A — model/effort tiering. Until this module existed every ARIA\nagent ran on the single most expensive setting and the frontmatter\n``model:``/``effort:`` fields were declared but never consumed at runtime.\n\nThe fix follows the \"scout-and-verify\" operator decision: the cheap tier\n(read-only scorers / scanners) flags candidates, the expensive tier\n(planners / arbiter / writers) decides and re-verifies and never trusts the\ncheap tier blindly. The agent frontmatter is the single source of truth for\nwhich tier each agent runs on. Two backends consume it:\n\n* Claude Code Agent dispatch honours ``model:`` natively.\n* The Claude Code CLI executor (``tools/aria-poc/claude_runtime.py``) resolves\n  ``model:`` to the ``--model`` alias via :func:`resolve_claude_model`.\n\nThis module is the only reader, so both consumers can never drift from the\nfrontmatter. Fail-safe by design: an unknown agent or a missing/invalid field\nresolves to the strongest SELECTED tier (``opus`` / ``max``). A silent cost\ndowngrade can therefore never be introduced by omission — only by an explicit,\nreviewable frontmatter edit. Operator decision 2026-09-12: ``fable`` stays a\nnamed tier (ordering, pricing) and is selected by nothing — no profile, no\nfrontmatter, no default; ``tests/invariants`` pins it. The same decision\nmade every tier a credit LEAF: an exhausted provider is requeued under its\ncooldown (``provider_cooldown``), never retried on a weaker tier, and the\nonly ladder left (``claude_runtime.AUTH_FAILOVER_TIER``) serves AUTH\nfailures across vendors.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom functools import lru_cache\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n\n# ORPHAN-HIGH-763 — `glm-5.3` (Z.ai) joins the dispatchable vocabulary.\n# It reaches the same `claude` binary through the provider swap Z.ai\n# publishes (ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=https://api.z.ai/api/anthropic), so this is\n# one more member of an existing vocabulary rather than a second runtime.\n# The lane still refuses API-key mode unless ARIA_ALLOW_CLAUDE_API_KEY_MODE=1\n# is set \"under a new policy\" (claude_runtime.assert_claude_policy_environment)\n# — that operator policy, not this constant, is what actually enables it.\nVALID_MODELS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\"opus\", \"sonnet\", \"haiku\", \"fable\", \"glm-5.3\"})\nVALID_EFFORTS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\"low\", \"medium\", \"high\", \"xhigh\", \"max\"})\n\nDEFAULT_MODEL: str = \"opus\"\n\n# E16 (ORPHAN-673) — model-tier write protection SSoT (operator rule\n# 2026-08-13): a weaker model must never delete or overwrite what a\n# stronger model authored, and models below the authoring floor must not\n# author agents at all. Strongest first; future stronger models are\n# PREPENDED here deliberately — never inferred.\n#\n# `glm-5.3` sits between fable and opus by OPERATOR DECISION (2026-08-20).\n# The consequence is deliberate and stated here because the ordering IS the\n# authority: ranking above `MIN_AGENT_AUTHORING_TIER` lets it author agents,\n# and opus may not overwrite what it authored. Note what admission COSTS: an\n# unlisted model resolves asymmetrically (weakest as actor, strongest as\n# target), so before this line glm output was protected from everyone and\n# could author nothing. Listing it trades that blanket protection for a\n# stated rank — which is the honest position once a model actually runs.\nMODEL_TIER_ORDER: tuple[str, ...] = (\"fable\", \"glm-5.3\", \"opus\", \"sonnet\", \"haiku\")\nMIN_AGENT_AUTHORING_TIER: str = \"opus\"\n\n\ndef model_tier_rank(model: str | None) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Rank in MODEL_TIER_ORDER; lower is stronger.\n\n    Unknown models resolve ASYMMETRICALLY by design: as a TARGET's author\n    they rank strongest (-1) — an agent stamped by a model this kernel\n    does not know is most plausibly a FUTURE, stronger model and must be\n    protected; as an ACTIVE actor they rank weakest (len) — a model that\n    cannot prove its tier may neither author nor modify. Callers pick the\n    side via the dedicated asserts below.\n    \"\"\"\n    if model in MODEL_TIER_ORDER:\n        return MODEL_TIER_ORDER.index(model)\n    return -1\n\n\ndef _active_model_rank(model: str | None) -> int:\n    if model in MODEL_TIER_ORDER:\n        return MODEL_TIER_ORDER.index(model)\n    return len(MODEL_TIER_ORDER)\n\n\ndef assert_model_may_author_agents(model: str | None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"E16 authoring floor: below MIN_AGENT_AUTHORING_TIER cannot author.\"\"\"\n    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError\n\n    if _active_model_rank(model) > MODEL_TIER_ORDER.index(MIN_AGENT_AUTHORING_TIER):\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"agent_authoring_tier_too_low: model={model!r} \"\n            f\"floor={MIN_AGENT_AUTHORING_TIER!r} order={MODEL_TIER_ORDER!r}\"\n        )\n\n\ndef assert_model_may_modify_agent(\n    *, active_model: str | None, target_authored_by: str | None\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"E16 write protection: weaker may not overwrite stronger's work.\n\n    An UNSTAMPED target (legacy agents predating authored_by_model) is\n    modifiable by any authoring-eligible model — the rule protects\n    provenance that exists, it does not invent provenance. The duel has\n    NO exception: a lower-tier duel winner still cannot supersede a\n    higher-tier agent directly; that path must fall to HUMAN_REQUIRED.\n    \"\"\"\n    from .tool_registry import GovernanceError\n\n    if not target_authored_by:\n        return\n    if _active_model_rank(active_model) > model_tier_rank(target_authored_by):\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            \"model_tier_insufficient_to_modify: \"\n            f\"active={active_model!r} target_authored_by={target_authored_by!r} \"\n            f\"order={MODEL_TIER_ORDER!r} (duel has no exception — escalate \"\n            \"to HUMAN_REQUIRED)\"\n        )\n# ORPHAN-HIGH-477 — ultracode depth. `max` is the CLI's deepest reasoning\n# level (verified against Claude Code 2.1.220: --effort low|medium|high|xhigh|max).\n# The fail-safe deliberately resolves UPWARD, so an unknown agent or an\n# unparseable frontmatter field can never quietly run shallower than policy.\nDEFAULT_EFFORT: str = \"max\"\n\n# Agents that hold write tools (Edit/Write/Bash) or author governance\n# artifacts MUST stay on the expensive tier — the cheap scout tier is for\n# read-only judgment only. Enforced by the model-tier invariant test so a\n# frontmatter edit can never quietly downgrade a writer.\nWRITE_TIER_AGENTS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\n    \"aria-implementer\",\n    \"aria-drafter\",\n    \"aria-prompt-writer\",\n    # Plan 030 / K3 — write-capable agents the jest mirror already pinned;\n    # the two sets must never diverge (ORPHAN-HIGH-285).\n    \"aria-acceptance-gap-fixer\",\n    \"aria-worker\",\n})\n\n_FRONTMATTER_RX = re.compile(r\"\\A---\\n(.*?)\\n---\", re.DOTALL)\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass AgentRuntimeProfile:\n    \"\"\"Resolved envelope for one agent. ``source`` records how it was derived.\n\n    Plan 032 Faz 032b — the envelope is more than a tier. When the agent's\n    frontmatter names a kernel-owned ``runtime_profile``, model/effort/tools/\n    write_scope/env_passthrough/external_writes come from\n    ``runtime_profiles.json`` (the authority the agent cannot edit) and the\n    frontmatter is only a mirror. ``source`` then reads ``kernel_profile``;\n    a mirror that disagrees with the kernel is recorded as\n    ``kernel_profile_mirror_drift`` and the KERNEL value is what runs.\n    Legacy agents without the key keep the frontmatter-only resolution.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    agent_name: str\n    model: str\n    effort: str\n    source: str  # \"kernel_profile\" | \"kernel_profile_mirror_drift\" | \"frontmatter\" | \"default_missing_file\" | \"default_invalid\"\n    profile_id: str | None = None\n    tools: tuple[str, ...] = ()\n    write_scope: tuple[str, ...] = ()\n    env_passthrough: tuple[str, ...] = ()\n    external_writes: bool = False\n    budget_usd_per_run: float | None = None\n    max_concurrent: int | None = None\n    runtime: str = \"claude\"\n\n    @property\n    def write_capable(self) -> bool:\n        from .runtime_profiles import WRITE_TOOLS\n\n        return bool(WRITE_TOOLS & set(self.tools))\n\n\ndef _repo_root() -> Path:\n    # <repo>/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/agent_runtime_profile.py\n    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\n\n\ndef _agents_dir(repo_root: Path | None) -> Path:\n    return (repo_root or _repo_root()) / \".claude\" / \"agents\"\n\n\ndef _find_agent_file(agent_name: str, repo_root: Path | None) -> Path | None:\n    base = _agents_dir(repo_root)\n    if not base.exists():\n        return None\n    direct = base / f\"{agent_name}.md\"\n    if direct.is_file():\n        return direct\n    matches = sorted(base.glob(f\"**/{agent_name}.md\"))\n    return matches[0] if matches else None\n\n\ndef _parse_frontmatter_field(text: str, field: str) -> str | None:\n    match = _FRONTMATTER_RX.match(text)\n    if not match:\n        return None\n    fm = match.group(1)\n    field_match = re.search(rf\"^{re.escape(field)}:\\s*(\\S+)\\s*$\", fm, re.MULTILINE)\n    if not field_match:\n        return None\n    return field_match.group(1).strip().strip(\"\\\"'\").lower()\n\n\n@lru_cache(maxsize=256)\ndef _read_profile_cached(agent_name: str, repo_root_str: str | None) -> AgentRuntimeProfile:\n    repo_root = Path(repo_root_str) if repo_root_str else None\n    path = _find_agent_file(agent_name, repo_root)\n    if path is None:\n        return AgentRuntimeProfile(agent_name, DEFAULT_MODEL, DEFAULT_EFFORT, \"default_missing_file\")\n    text = path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    raw_model = _parse_frontmatter_field(text, \"model\")\n    raw_effort = _parse_frontmatter_field(text, \"effort\")\n    # Plan 032 Faz 032b — a kernel-owned profile is the authority when named.\n    from .runtime_profiles import RUNTIME_PROFILE_FRONTMATTER_KEY, load_runtime_profiles\n\n    profile_id = _parse_frontmatter_field(text, RUNTIME_PROFILE_FRONTMATTER_KEY)\n    if profile_id:\n        kernel = load_runtime_profiles().get(profile_id)\n        if kernel is None:\n            # Naming an unknown profile is an invalid frontmatter, and the\n            # fail-safe is the same as any other invalid field: the most\n            # expensive read-only tier, with no envelope widening.\n            return AgentRuntimeProfile(agent_name, DEFAULT_MODEL, DEFAULT_EFFORT, \"default_invalid\")\n        source = \"kernel_profile\"\n        if raw_model != kernel.model or raw_effort != kernel.effort:\n            source = \"kernel_profile_mirror_drift\"\n        return AgentRuntimeProfile(\n            agent_name, kernel.model, kernel.effort, source,\n            profile_id=kernel.profile_id,\n            tools=kernel.tools,\n            write_scope=kernel.write_scope,\n            env_passthrough=kernel.env_passthrough,\n            external_writes=kernel.external_writes,\n            budget_usd_per_run=kernel.budget_usd_per_run,\n            max_concurrent=kernel.max_concurrent,\n            runtime=kernel.runtime,\n        )\n    model = raw_model if raw_model in VALID_MODELS else DEFAULT_MODEL\n    effort = raw_effort if raw_effort in VALID_EFFORTS else DEFAULT_EFFORT\n    source = \"frontmatter\"\n    if raw_model not in VALID_MODELS or raw_effort not in VALID_EFFORTS:\n        source = \"default_invalid\"\n    return AgentRuntimeProfile(agent_name, model, effort, source)\n\n\ndef read_agent_runtime_profile(\n    agent_name: str,\n    *,\n    repo_root: str | Path | None = None,\n) -> AgentRuntimeProfile:\n    \"\"\"Resolve the (model, effort) tier declared in an agent's frontmatter.\n\n    Fail-safe: unknown agent or invalid/missing field → most expensive tier.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(agent_name, str) or not agent_name.strip():\n        return AgentRuntimeProfile(str(agent_name), DEFAULT_MODEL, DEFAULT_EFFORT, \"default_invalid\")\n    root_str = str(Path(repo_root).resolve()) if repo_root is not None else None\n    return _read_profile_cached(agent_name.strip(), root_str)\n\n\ndef resolve_claude_model(\n    agent_name: str,\n    *,\n    repo_root: str | Path | None = None,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Claude Code CLI executor lever: resolve an agent's frontmatter\n    ``model`` tier to the ``--model`` alias the CLI consumes. Fail-safe to\n    the most expensive tier for unknown agents or invalid fields.\"\"\"\n    return read_agent_runtime_profile(agent_name, repo_root=repo_root).model\n\n\ndef resolve_claude_effort(\n    agent_name: str,\n    *,\n    repo_root: str | Path | None = None,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Claude Code CLI executor lever: resolve an agent's frontmatter\n    ``effort`` tier to the ``--effort`` level the CLI consumes. Shares the\n    fail-safe path with :func:`resolve_claude_model` (most expensive tier).\"\"\"\n    return read_agent_runtime_profile(agent_name, repo_root=repo_root).effort\n\n# E16 — the provenance stamp lives WITH the tier order: stamping is a\n# governance concern of the runtime-profile SSoT, not draft rendering\n# (agent_genesis is barred from markdown/frontmatter literals by\n# I-V3-12c, and rightly so — the kernel governs provenance, the drafter\n# renders content).\ndef parse_authored_by_model(text: str) -> str | None:\n    match = re.search(\n        r\"^authored_by_model:\\s*([A-Za-z0-9._-]+)\\s*$\", text, flags=re.MULTILINE\n    )\n    return match.group(1) if match else None\n\n\ndef stamp_authored_by_model(body: str, model: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Kernel-injected provenance stamp (E16). A drafter-supplied value\n    is OVERWRITTEN — the stamp is measured at mint, never claimed.\"\"\"\n    if parse_authored_by_model(body) is not None:\n        return re.sub(\n            r\"^authored_by_model:\\s*[A-Za-z0-9._-]+\\s*$\",\n            f\"authored_by_model: {model}\",\n            body,\n            count=1,\n            flags=re.MULTILINE,\n        )\n    if body.startswith(\"---\\n\"):\n        return body.replace(\"---\\n\", f\"---\\nauthored_by_model: {model}\\n\", 1)\n    return f\"---\\nauthored_by_model: {model}\\n---\\n{body}\"\n","numLines":313,"startLine":1,"totalLines":313}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Ea99CkKyTKmnpt9jvTMkST","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Plan 032 Faz 032b — kernel-owned runtime profiles (the agent envelope SSoT).\n2\t\n3\tWHY: until this module, the only statement of what an agent may run as was\n4\tits own markdown frontmatter (``model:``, ``effort:``, ``tools:``), and only\n5\t``model``/``effort`` were consumed at runtime — ``tools:`` was prose the\n6\tClaude Code loader honoured for native sub-agent dispatch and the CLI\n7\texecutor never passed on. An agent that can edit files can, in principle,\n8\tedit the file that describes its own permissions; the second review of\n9\t2026-09-02 named that the authority must not live where the agent can reach.\n10\t\n11\tWHAT: ``data/runtime_profiles.json`` is the authority. It lives under\n12\t``aria-kernel/aria_kernel/`` — inside ``implementation_safety.READONLY_PATHS``\n13\t(ro-bind at the syscall level for every write-capable spawn) and inside the\n14\t``docs/aria/CURRENT_STATE.md`` authority hash — so changing an envelope is a\n15\tkernel change that goes through the kernel-change lane. Agent markdown\n16\tREFERENCES a profile (``runtime_profile: <id>``) and MIRRORS model/effort/tools\n17\tso the native loader keeps working; :func:`verify_agent_mirrors` refuses a\n18\tmirror that disagrees with the kernel.\n19\t\n20\tThe envelope a profile describes is enforced by three consumers:\n21\t\n22\t* ``agent_runtime_profile`` resolves model/effort from the profile (and the\n23\t  rest of the envelope) for both executors;\n24\t* ``claude_runtime`` derives ``--disallowedTools`` from :func:`disallowed_tools_for`\n25\t  and builds the spawn environment from ``env_passthrough``;\n26\t* ``implementation_safety.wrap_bash_in_sandbox`` binds only ``write_scope``\n27\t  read-write.\n28\t\n29\tClosed vocabularies on purpose: an unknown key, tool, model or effort in the\n30\tJSON is a load-time refusal, never a silently ignored field.\n31\t\"\"\"\n32\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n33\t\n34\timport re\n35\t\n36\timport json\n37\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n38\tfrom functools import lru_cache\n39\tfrom pathlib import Path\n40\tfrom typing import Any\n41\t\n42\tfrom .tool_registry import GovernanceError\n43\t\n44\tRUNTIME_PROFILES_SCHEMA = \"aria/runtime-profiles/v1\"\n45\tRUNTIME_PROFILES_FILENAME = \"runtime_profiles.json\"\n46\t# The frontmatter key an agent markdown uses to name its envelope.\n47\tRUNTIME_PROFILE_FRONTMATTER_KEY = \"runtime_profile\"\n48\t\n49\t# The Claude Code tools an ARIA agent may ever be granted. Mirrors the jest\n50\t# `agent-frontmatter-schema` whitelist; anything outside it is refused at load.\n51\tCLAUDE_TOOL_UNIVERSE: tuple[str, ...] = (\n52\t    \"Read\", \"Grep\", \"Glob\", \"Edit\", \"Write\", \"MultiEdit\", \"NotebookEdit\",\n53\t    \"Bash\", \"Agent\", \"WebFetch\", \"WebSearch\", \"TodoWrite\",\n54\t    # ARIA-HIGH-162 — tools the CLI (2.1.278) exposes that no ARIA profile\n55\t    # grants and the deny list never named: a skill loader, a tool-schema\n56\t    # loader, a worktree creator (a filesystem write), an artifact publisher\n57\t    # (network egress through the CLI's own auth) and a session messenger.\n58\t    # Under bwrap the gap was harmless; the read shape's `--tools` allowlist\n59\t    # closes it first, and naming them here keeps the deny list the belt.\n60\t    \"Skill\", \"ToolSearch\", \"EnterWorktree\", \"Artifact\", \"SendMessage\",\n61\t)\n62\t# Tools that never appear in an ARIA profile and are therefore denied on every\n63\t# spawn: network reach through tools is not something a repo-scoped agent needs\n64\t# (the CLI's own API traffic is not a tool call).\n65\tALWAYS_DENIED_TOOLS: tuple[str, ...] = (\"WebFetch\", \"WebSearch\")\n66\t# Write tools — a profile carrying any of these is a WRITE-TIER profile.\n67\tWRITE_TOOLS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\"Edit\", \"Write\", \"MultiEdit\", \"NotebookEdit\", \"Bash\"})\n68\t\n69\t# Claude permission rules that close the external-write channels while a\n70\t# profile has ``external_writes: false``. Scoped `Bash(...)` rules deny only\n71\t# matching calls; the tool itself stays available for tests/lint/git-local.\n72\t# Documented to bind in every permission mode, bypassPermissions included.\n73\tEXTERNAL_WRITE_DENY_RULES: tuple[str, ...] = (\n74\t    \"Bash(git push*)\",\n75\t    \"Bash(gh pr create*)\",\n76\t    \"Bash(gh pr comment*)\",\n77\t    \"Bash(gh pr edit*)\",\n78\t    \"Bash(gh pr merge*)\",\n79\t    \"Bash(gh issue*)\",\n80\t    \"Bash(gh api*)\",\n81\t    \"Bash(gh release*)\",\n82\t    \"Bash(gh workflow*)\",\n83\t)\n84\t\n85\t_PROFILE_KEYS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\n86\t    \"description\", \"model\", \"effort\", \"tools\", \"write_scope\", \"env_passthrough\",\n87\t    \"external_writes\", \"budget_usd_per_run\", \"max_concurrent\",\n88\t    \"mcp_servers\", \"runtime\",\n89\t})\n90\t_OPTIONAL_KEYS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\"description\", \"mcp_servers\", \"runtime\"})\n91\t\n92\t\n93\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n94\tclass RuntimeProfile:\n95\t    profile_id: str\n96\t    model: str\n97\t    effort: str\n98\t    tools: tuple[str, ...]\n99\t    write_scope: tuple[str, ...]\n100\t    env_passthrough: tuple[str, ...]\n101\t    external_writes: bool\n102\t    budget_usd_per_run: float\n103\t    max_concurrent: int\n104\t    description: str = \"\"\n105\t    # Plan 032 Faz 032g — MCP servers (registry names) this profile may load.\n106\t    mcp_servers: tuple[str, ...] = ()\n107\t    runtime: str = \"claude\"\n108\t\n109\t    @property\n110\t    def write_capable(self) -> bool:\n111\t        return bool(WRITE_TOOLS & set(self.tools))\n112\t\n113\t\n114\tdef profiles_path() -> Path:\n115\t    return Path(__file__).resolve().parent / \"data\" / RUNTIME_PROFILES_FILENAME\n116\t\n117\t\n118\tdef _validate_profile(profile_id: str, raw: dict[str, Any]) -> RuntimeProfile:\n119\t    from .agent_runtime_profile import VALID_EFFORTS, VALID_MODELS\n120\t\n121\t    unknown = sorted(set(raw) - _PROFILE_KEYS)\n122\t    missing = sorted(_PROFILE_KEYS - _OPTIONAL_KEYS - set(raw))\n123\t    if unknown or missing:\n124\t        raise GovernanceError(\n125\t            f\"runtime_profile_shape:{profile_id}:unknown={unknown}:missing={missing}\"\n126\t        )\n127\t    runtime = raw.get(\"runtime\", \"claude\")\n128\t    if runtime not in (\"claude\", \"codex\"):\n129\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_runtime:{profile_id}:{runtime!r}\")\n130\t    if runtime == \"codex\":\n131\t        if raw[\"model\"] != \"gpt-6-astra\":\n132\t            raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_model:{profile_id}:{raw['model']!r}\")\n133\t        if raw[\"effort\"] != \"ultra\":\n134\t            raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_effort:{profile_id}:{raw['effort']!r}\")\n135\t    else:\n136\t        if raw[\"model\"] not in VALID_MODELS:\n137\t            raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_model:{profile_id}:{raw['model']!r}\")\n138\t        if raw[\"effort\"] not in VALID_EFFORTS:\n139\t            raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_effort:{profile_id}:{raw['effort']!r}\")\n140\t    tools = tuple(str(t) for t in raw[\"tools\"])\n141\t    bad_tools = sorted(set(tools) - set(CLAUDE_TOOL_UNIVERSE))\n142\t    if not tools or bad_tools or len(set(tools)) != len(tools):\n143\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_tools:{profile_id}:{bad_tools or tools}\")\n144\t    if set(tools) & set(ALWAYS_DENIED_TOOLS):\n145\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_tools_never_granted:{profile_id}\")\n146\t    write_scope = tuple(str(s) for s in raw[\"write_scope\"])\n147\t    if write_scope and not (WRITE_TOOLS & set(tools)):\n148\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_write_scope_without_write_tools:{profile_id}\")\n149\t    if not write_scope and ({\"Edit\", \"Write\", \"MultiEdit\", \"NotebookEdit\"} & set(tools)):\n150\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_edit_tools_without_write_scope:{profile_id}\")\n151\t    passthrough = tuple(str(name) for name in raw[\"env_passthrough\"])\n152\t    for name in passthrough:\n153\t        if not name or not name.replace(\"_\", \"\").isalnum() or name != name.upper():\n154\t            raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_env_name:{profile_id}:{name!r}\")\n155\t    if not isinstance(raw[\"external_writes\"], bool):\n156\t        raise Refuses (GovernanceError) on any\n206\t    unknown key/tool/model — a profile file that half-loads is worse than none.\"\"\"\n207\t    return dict(_load_cached(str(Path(path) if path else profiles_path())))\n208\t\n209\t\n210\tdef profile_by_id(profile_id: str, *, path: str | Path | None = None) -> RuntimeProfile:\n211\t    profiles = load_runtime_profiles(path)\n212\t    try:\n213\t        return profiles[profile_id]\n214\t    except KeyError as exc:\n215\t        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_unknown:{profile_id}\") from exc\n216\t\n217\t\n218\tdef disallowed_tools_for(profile: RuntimeProfile) -> tuple[str, ...]:\n219\t    \"\"\"The ``--disallowedTools`` list a spawn under ``profile`` carries.\n220\t\n221\t    Bare tool names REMOVE tools the profile does not grant (the universe minus\n222\t    the grant, always including the never-granted set); scoped rules close the\n223\t    external-write channels while ``external_writes`` is False. Deterministic\n224\t    order so the argv is stable across runs and pinnable by tests.\n225\t    \"\"\"\n226\t    granted = set(profile.tools)\n227\t    removed = [tool for tool in CLAUDE_TOOL_UNIVERSE if tool not in granted]\n228\t    rules: list[str] = list(removed)\n229\t    if not profile.external_writes and \"Bash\" in granted:\n230\t        rules.extend(EXTERNAL_WRITE_DENY_RULES)\n231\t    return tuple(rules)\n232\t\n233\t\n234\tdef verify_agent_mirrors(*, repo_root: str | Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n235\t    \"\"\"Every ``aria-*`` agent markdown names a profile and mirrors it exactly.\n236\t\n237\t    Returns one row per defect; empty means every mirror agrees with the\n238\t    kernel. Used by the Plan 032 invariant test and available to `doctor`.\n239\t    \"\"\"\n240\t    from .agent_runtime_profile import _find_agent_file, _parse_frontmatter_field  # noqa: PLC0415\n241\t\n242\t    root = Path(repo_root)\n243\t    agents_dir = root / \".claude\" / \"agents\"\n244\t    defects: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n245\t    profiles = load_runtime_profiles()\n246\t    for path in sorted(agents_dir.glob(\"aria-*.md\")):\n247\t        text = path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n248\t        rel = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()\n249\t        profile_id = \"runtime_profile_mirror_drift\", \"field\": \"tools\",\n270\t                \"mirror\": sorted(mirror_tools), \"kernel\": sorted(profile.tools),\n271\t            })\n272\t    return {token.strip() for token in field.group(1).split(\",\") if token.strip()}\n285\t\n286\t\n287\t__all__ = [\n288\t    \"ALWAYS_DENIED_TOOLS\",\n289\t    \"CLAUDE_TOOL_UNIVERSE\",\n290\t    \"EXTERNAL_WRITE_DENY_RULES\",\n291\t    \"RUNTIME_PROFILES_SCHEMA\",\n292\t    \"RUNTIME_PROFILE_FRONTMATTER_KEY\",\n293\t    \"RuntimeProfile\",\n294\t    \"WRITE_TOOLS\",\n295\t    \"disallowed_tools_for\",\n296\t    \"load_runtime_profiles\",\n297\t    \"profile_by_id\",\n298\t    \"profiles_path\",\n299\t    \"verify_agent_mirrors\",\n300\t]\n301\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"15e2d44a-5660-4a51-a46d-eca1518eadb6","uuid":"f68b03b6-999d-47a4-a11f-85b4458c2a18","timestamp":"2026-09-25T10:46:31.085Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-consensus-arbiter-00bb0e8ea72e/aria-kernel/aria_kernel/runtime_profiles.py","content":"\"\"\"Plan 032 Faz 032b — kernel-owned runtime profiles (the agent envelope SSoT).\n\nWHY: until this module, the only statement of what an agent may run as was\nits own markdown frontmatter (``model:``, ``effort:``, ``tools:``), and only\n``model``/``effort`` were consumed at runtime — ``tools:`` was prose the\nClaude Code loader honoured for native sub-agent dispatch and the CLI\nexecutor never passed on. An agent that can edit files can, in principle,\nedit the file that describes its own permissions; the second review of\n2026-09-02 named that the authority must not live where the agent can reach.\n\nWHAT: ``data/runtime_profiles.json`` is the authority. It lives under\n``aria-kernel/aria_kernel/`` — inside ``implementation_safety.READONLY_PATHS``\n(ro-bind at the syscall level for every write-capable spawn) and inside the\n``docs/aria/CURRENT_STATE.md`` authority hash — so changing an envelope is a\nkernel change that goes through the kernel-change lane. Agent markdown\nREFERENCES a profile (``runtime_profile: <id>``) and MIRRORS model/effort/tools\nso the native loader keeps working; :func:`verify_agent_mirrors` refuses a\nmirror that disagrees with the kernel.\n\nThe envelope a profile describes is enforced by three consumers:\n\n* ``agent_runtime_profile`` resolves model/effort from the profile (and the\n  rest of the envelope) for both executors;\n* ``claude_runtime`` derives ``--disallowedTools`` from :func:`disallowed_tools_for`\n  and builds the spawn environment from ``env_passthrough``;\n* ``implementation_safety.wrap_bash_in_sandbox`` binds only ``write_scope``\n  read-write.\n\nClosed vocabularies on purpose: an unknown key, tool, model or effort in the\nJSON is a load-time refusal, never a silently ignored field.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\n\nimport json\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom functools import lru_cache\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom .tool_registry import GovernanceError\n\nRUNTIME_PROFILES_SCHEMA = \"aria/runtime-profiles/v1\"\nRUNTIME_PROFILES_FILENAME = \"runtime_profiles.json\"\n# The frontmatter key an agent markdown uses to name its envelope.\nRUNTIME_PROFILE_FRONTMATTER_KEY = \"runtime_profile\"\n\n# The Claude Code tools an ARIA agent may ever be granted. Mirrors the jest\n# `agent-frontmatter-schema` whitelist; anything outside it is refused at load.\nCLAUDE_TOOL_UNIVERSE: tuple[str, ...] = (\n    \"Read\", \"Grep\", \"Glob\", \"Edit\", \"Write\", \"MultiEdit\", \"NotebookEdit\",\n    \"Bash\", \"Agent\", \"WebFetch\", \"WebSearch\", \"TodoWrite\",\n    # ARIA-HIGH-162 — tools the CLI (2.1.278) exposes that no ARIA profile\n    # grants and the deny list never named: a skill loader, a tool-schema\n    # loader, a worktree creator (a filesystem write), an artifact publisher\n    # (network egress through the CLI's own auth) and a session messenger.\n    # Under bwrap the gap was harmless; the read shape's `--tools` allowlist\n    # closes it first, and naming them here keeps the deny list the belt.\n    \"Skill\", \"ToolSearch\", \"EnterWorktree\", \"Artifact\", \"SendMessage\",\n)\n# Tools that never appear in an ARIA profile and are therefore denied on every\n# spawn: network reach through tools is not something a repo-scoped agent needs\n# (the CLI's own API traffic is not a tool call).\nALWAYS_DENIED_TOOLS: tuple[str, ...] = (\"WebFetch\", \"WebSearch\")\n# Write tools — a profile carrying any of these is a WRITE-TIER profile.\nWRITE_TOOLS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\"Edit\", \"Write\", \"MultiEdit\", \"NotebookEdit\", \"Bash\"})\n\n# Claude permission rules that close the external-write channels while a\n# profile has ``external_writes: false``. Scoped `Bash(...)` rules deny only\n# matching calls; the tool itself stays available for tests/lint/git-local.\n# Documented to bind in every permission mode, bypassPermissions included.\nEXTERNAL_WRITE_DENY_RULES: tuple[str, ...] = (\n    \"Bash(git push*)\",\n    \"Bash(gh pr create*)\",\n    \"Bash(gh pr comment*)\",\n    \"Bash(gh pr edit*)\",\n    \"Bash(gh pr merge*)\",\n    \"Bash(gh issue*)\",\n    \"Bash(gh api*)\",\n    \"Bash(gh release*)\",\n    \"Bash(gh workflow*)\",\n)\n\n_PROFILE_KEYS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\n    \"description\", \"model\", \"effort\", \"tools\", \"write_scope\", \"env_passthrough\",\n    \"external_writes\", \"budget_usd_per_run\", \"max_concurrent\",\n    \"mcp_servers\", \"runtime\",\n})\n_OPTIONAL_KEYS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\"description\", \"mcp_servers\", \"runtime\"})\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass RuntimeProfile:\n    profile_id: str\n    model: str\n    effort: str\n    tools: tuple[str, ...]\n    write_scope: tuple[str, ...]\n    env_passthrough: tuple[str, ...]\n    external_writes: bool\n    budget_usd_per_run: float\n    max_concurrent: int\n    description: str = \"\"\n    # Plan 032 Faz 032g — MCP servers (registry names) this profile may load.\n    mcp_servers: tuple[str, ...] = ()\n    runtime: str = \"claude\"\n\n    @property\n    def write_capable(self) -> bool:\n        return bool(WRITE_TOOLS & set(self.tools))\n\n\ndef profiles_path() -> Path:\n    return Path(__file__).resolve().parent / \"data\" / RUNTIME_PROFILES_FILENAME\n\n\ndef _validate_profile(profile_id: str, raw: dict[str, Any]) -> RuntimeProfile:\n    from .agent_runtime_profile import VALID_EFFORTS, VALID_MODELS\n\n    unknown = sorted(set(raw) - _PROFILE_KEYS)\n    missing = sorted(_PROFILE_KEYS - _OPTIONAL_KEYS - set(raw))\n    if unknown or missing:\n        raise GovernanceError(\n            f\"runtime_profile_shape:{profile_id}:unknown={unknown}:missing={missing}\"\n        )\n    runtime = raw.get(\"runtime\", \"claude\")\n    if runtime not in (\"claude\", \"codex\"):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_runtime:{profile_id}:{runtime!r}\")\n    if runtime == \"codex\":\n        if raw[\"model\"] != \"gpt-6-astra\":\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_model:{profile_id}:{raw['model']!r}\")\n        if raw[\"effort\"] != \"ultra\":\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_effort:{profile_id}:{raw['effort']!r}\")\n    else:\n        if raw[\"model\"] not in VALID_MODELS:\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_model:{profile_id}:{raw['model']!r}\")\n        if raw[\"effort\"] not in VALID_EFFORTS:\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_effort:{profile_id}:{raw['effort']!r}\")\n    tools = tuple(str(t) for t in raw[\"tools\"])\n    bad_tools = sorted(set(tools) - set(CLAUDE_TOOL_UNIVERSE))\n    if not tools or bad_tools or len(set(tools)) != len(tools):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_tools:{profile_id}:{bad_tools or tools}\")\n    if set(tools) & set(ALWAYS_DENIED_TOOLS):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_tools_never_granted:{profile_id}\")\n    write_scope = tuple(str(s) for s in raw[\"write_scope\"])\n    if write_scope and not (WRITE_TOOLS & set(tools)):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_write_scope_without_write_tools:{profile_id}\")\n    if not write_scope and ({\"Edit\", \"Write\", \"MultiEdit\", \"NotebookEdit\"} & set(tools)):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_edit_tools_without_write_scope:{profile_id}\")\n    passthrough = tuple(str(name) for name in raw[\"env_passthrough\"])\n    for name in passthrough:\n        if not name or not name.replace(\"_\", \"\").isalnum() or name != name.upper():\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_env_name:{profile_id}:{name!r}\")\n    if not isinstance(raw[\"external_writes\"], bool):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_external_writes:{profile_id}\")\n    budget = raw[\"budget_usd_per_run\"]\n    if not isinstance(budget, (int, float)) or isinstance(budget, bool) or budget <= 0:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_budget:{profile_id}\")\n    concurrency = raw[\"max_concurrent\"]\n    if not isinstance(concurrency, int) or isinstance(concurrency, bool) or concurrency < 1:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_concurrency:{profile_id}\")\n    mcp_servers = tuple(str(n) for n in (raw.get(\"mcp_servers\") or []))\n    if len(set(mcp_servers)) != len(mcp_servers) or any(not re.match(r\"^[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{1,31}$\", n) for n in mcp_servers):\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_mcp_servers:{profile_id}:{mcp_servers}\")\n    return RuntimeProfile(\n        profile_id=profile_id,\n        model=str(raw[\"model\"]),\n        effort=str(raw[\"effort\"]),\n        tools=tools,\n        write_scope=write_scope,\n        env_passthrough=passthrough,\n        external_writes=bool(raw[\"external_writes\"]),\n        budget_usd_per_run=float(budget),\n        max_concurrent=int(concurrency),\n        description=str(raw.get(\"description\") or \"\"),\n        mcp_servers=mcp_servers,\n        runtime=runtime,\n    )\n\n\n@lru_cache(maxsize=4)\ndef _load_cached(path_str: str) -> dict[str, RuntimeProfile]:\n    path = Path(path_str)\n    try:\n        raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profiles_unreadable:{path.as_posix()}:{type(exc).__name__}\") from exc\n    if not isinstance(raw, dict) or raw.get(\"$schema\") != RUNTIME_PROFILES_SCHEMA:\n        raise GovernanceError(\"runtime_profiles_schema\")\n    profiles = raw.get(\"profiles\")\n    if not isinstance(profiles, dict) or not profiles:\n        raise GovernanceError(\"runtime_profiles_empty\")\n    out: dict[str, RuntimeProfile] = {}\n    for profile_id, body in profiles.items():\n        if not isinstance(profile_id, str) or not profile_id.replace(\"_\", \"\").isalnum():\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_id:{profile_id!r}\")\n        if not isinstance(body, dict):\n            raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_body:{profile_id}\")\n        out[profile_id] = _validate_profile(profile_id, body)\n    return out\n\n\ndef load_runtime_profiles(path: str | Path | None = None) -> dict[str, RuntimeProfile]:\n    \"\"\"Every declared profile, validated. Refuses (GovernanceError) on any\n    unknown key/tool/model — a profile file that half-loads is worse than none.\"\"\"\n    return dict(_load_cached(str(Path(path) if path else profiles_path())))\n\n\ndef profile_by_id(profile_id: str, *, path: str | Path | None = None) -> RuntimeProfile:\n    profiles = load_runtime_profiles(path)\n    try:\n        return profiles[profile_id]\n    except KeyError as exc:\n        raise GovernanceError(f\"runtime_profile_unknown:{profile_id}\") from exc\n\n\ndef disallowed_tools_for(profile: RuntimeProfile) -> tuple[str, ...]:\n    \"\"\"The ``--disallowedTools`` list a spawn under ``profile`` carries.\n\n    Bare tool names REMOVE tools the profile does not grant (the universe minus\n    the grant, always including the never-granted set); scoped rules close the\n    external-write channels while ``external_writes`` is False. Deterministic\n    order so the argv is stable across runs and pinnable by tests.\n    \"\"\"\n    granted = set(profile.tools)\n    removed = [tool for tool in CLAUDE_TOOL_UNIVERSE if tool not in granted]\n    rules: list[str] = list(removed)\n    if not profile.external_writes and \"Bash\" in granted:\n        rules.extend(EXTERNAL_WRITE_DENY_RULES)\n    return tuple(rules)\n\n\ndef verify_agent_mirrors(*, repo_root: str | Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Every ``aria-*`` agent markdown names a profile and mirrors it exactly.\n\n    Returns one row per defect; empty means every mirror agrees with the\n    kernel. Used by the Plan 032 invariant test and available to `doctor`.\n    \"\"\"\n    from .agent_runtime_profile import _find_agent_file, _parse_frontmatter_field  # noqa: PLC0415\n\n    root = Path(repo_root)\n    agents_dir = root / \".claude\" / \"agents\"\n    defects: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    profiles = load_runtime_profiles()\n    for path in sorted(agents_dir.glob(\"aria-*.md\")):\n        text = path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        rel = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()\n        profile_id = _parse_frontmatter_field(text, RUNTIME_PROFILE_FRONTMATTER_KEY)\n        if not profile_id:\n            defects.append({\"ref\": rel, \"reason\": \"runtime_profile_missing\"})\n            continue\n        profile = profiles.get(profile_id)\n        if profile is None:\n            defects.append({\"ref\": rel, \"reason\": \"runtime_profile_unknown\", \"profile\": profile_id})\n            continue\n        mirror_model = _parse_frontmatter_field(text, \"model\")\n        mirror_effort = _parse_frontmatter_field(text, \"effort\")\n        if mirror_model != profile.model or mirror_effort != profile.effort:\n            defects.append({\n                \"ref\": rel, \"reason\": \"runtime_profile_mirror_drift\",\n                \"field\": \"model/effort\",\n                \"mirror\": f\"{mirror_model}/{mirror_effort}\",\n                \"kernel\": f\"{profile.model}/{profile.effort}\",\n            })\n        mirror_tools = _parse_frontmatter_tools(text)\n        if mirror_tools != set(profile.tools):\n            defects.append({\n                \"ref\": rel, \"reason\": \"runtime_profile_mirror_drift\", \"field\": \"tools\",\n                \"mirror\": sorted(mirror_tools), \"kernel\": sorted(profile.tools),\n            })\n    return defects\n\n\ndef _parse_frontmatter_tools(text: str) -> set[str]:\n    import re\n\n    match = re.match(r\"\\A---\\n(.*?)\\n---\", text, re.DOTALL)\n    if not match:\n        return set()\n    field = re.search(r\"^tools:\\s*(.+?)\\s*$\", match.group(1), re.MULTILINE)\n    if not field:\n        return set()\n    return {token.strip() for token in field.group(1).split(\",\") if token.strip()}\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"ALWAYS_DENIED_TOOLS\",\n    \"CLAUDE_TOOL_UNIVERSE\",\n    \"EXTERNAL_WRITE_DENY_RULES\",\n    \"RUNTIME_PROFILES_SCHEMA\",\n    \"RUNTIME_PROFILE_FRONTMATTER_KEY\",\n    \"RuntimeProfile\",\n    \"WRITE_TOOLS\",\n    \"disallowed_tools_for\",\n    \"load_runtime_profiles\",\n    \"profile_by_id\",\n    \"profiles_path\",\n    \"verify_agent_mirrors\",\n]\n","numLines":301,"startLine":1,"totalLines":301}}}

Pinned here so the doctor and the lanes cannot drift;\n32\t# tests/test_doctor.py compares the three literals.\n33\tCLAUDE_CLI_VERSION_FLOOR = \"2.1.221\"\n34\t\n35\tCHECK_STATUSES: tuple[str, ...] = (\"ok\", \"warn\", \"fail\")\n36\tDOCTOR_EXIT_HEALTHY = 0\n37\tDOCTOR_EXIT_UNHEALTHY = 3\n38\t\n39\t\n40\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n41\tclass DoctorCheck:\n42\t    name: str\n43\t    status: str\n44\t 032 Faz 032a — ``aria-kernel doctor``: one readout of ARIA's own health.\n\nWHY: every fact below already existed behind its own verb (`integrity\nverify`, `runtime verify-artifacts`, `breaker status`, `cost-breaker status`,\n`autonomy status`, the habitat probe, the funnel detector) and nothing put\nthem side by side. On 2026-09-02 the operator learned that the nightly had\nbeen dying on one oversized ledger row, that the plan ledger was missing from\nthe store, and that the executor's queue was all age-expired, by reading\nthree workflow logs and two ledgers by hand. A doctor is the report that\nwould have said so in one screen.\n\nWHAT IT IS NOT: it decides nothing and writes nothing. Each check is a\npure read that returns ``ok`` | ``warn`` | ``fail`` with a reason; the exit\ncode is derived (0 healthy, 3 at least one ``fail``) the way the runtime\nsupervisor already reports (SuccessExitStatus=0 3). A check that cannot run\nreports ``warn`` with the exception NAME, never a stack — an unreadable\norgan is a finding, not a crash.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nimport subprocess\nfrom dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Callable\n\nfrom .tool_registry import ensure_tools_dir_readonly\n\n# The floor both live lanes' preflight steps enforce (aria-auto-cycle.yml /\n# aria-agent-executor.yml `REQUIRED_CLAUDE_VERSION`) and provision_runner.sh\n# names as CLAUDE_FLOOR. Pinned here so the doctor and the lanes cannot drift;\n# tests/test_doctor.py compares the three literals.\nCLAUDE_CLI_VERSION_FLOOR = \"2.1.221\"\n\nCHECK_STATUSES: tuple[str, ...] = (\"ok\", \"warn\", \"fail\")\nDOCTOR_EXIT_HEALTHY = 0\nDOCTOR_EXIT_UNHEALTHY = 3\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass DoctorCheck:\n    name: str\n    status: str\n    reason: str = \"\"\n    detail: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass DoctorReport:\n    checks: tuple[DoctorCheck, ...]\n    tools_dir: str\n    workspace_root: str\n\n    @property\n    def healthy(self) -> bool:\n        return all(check.status != \"fail\" for check in self.checks)\n\n    @property\n    def exit_code(self) -> int:\n        return DOCTOR_EXIT_HEALTHY if self.healthy else DOCTOR_EXIT_UNHEALTHY\n\n    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        return {\n            \"schema_version\": 1,\n            \"healthy\": self.healthy,\n            \"exit_code\": self.exit_code,\n            \"tools_dir\": self.tools_dir,\n            \"workspace_root\": self.workspace_root,\n            \"summary\": {\n                status: sum(1 for check in self.checks if check.status == status)\n                for status in CHECK_STATUSES\n            },\n            \"checks\": [asdict(check) for check in self.checks],\n        }\n\n\ndef _version_tuple(text: str) -> tuple[int, ...]:\n    digits: list[int] = []\n    for part in text.strip().split(\".\")[:3]:\n        number = \"\"","numLines":80,"startLine":1,"totalLines":663}}}

== \"ok\" else \"single_provider_no_striping\"\n    return DoctorCheck(\"providers\", status, reason, {\"providers\": providers})\n\n\ndef _check_sandbox() -> DoctorCheck:\n    from .implementation_safety import sandbox_backend\n\n    backend = sandbox_backend()\n    if backend:\n        return DoctorCheck(\"sandbox_backend\", \"ok\", detail={\"backend\": backend})\n    return DoctorCheck(\n        \"sandbox_backend\", \"fail\", \"no_sandbox_backend_write_capable_spawns_refused\",\n    )\n\n\ndef _check_claude_cli(*, floor: str = CLAUDE_CLI_VERSION_FLOOR) -> DoctorCheck:\n    binary = shutil.which(\"claude\")\n    if binary is None:\n        return DoctorCheck(\"claude_cli\", \"fail\", \"claude_binary_missing\", {\"floor\": floor})\n    proc = subprocess.run(\n        [binary, \"--version\"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20, check=False,\n    )\n    raw = (proc.stdout or proc.stderr or \"\").strip()\n    token = raw.split()[0] if raw else \"\"\n    version = _version_tuple(token)\n    detail = {\"binary\": binary, \"reported\": raw[:80], \"floor\": floor}\n    if proc.returncode != 0 or not version:\n        return DoctorCheck(\"claude_cli\", \"warn\", \"claude_version_unreadable\", detail)\n    if version < _version_tuple(floor):\n        return DoctorCheck(\"claude_cli\", \"fail\", f\"claude_below_floor:{token}<{floor}\", detail)\n    return DoctorCheck(\"claude_cli\", \"ok\", detail=detail)\n\n\ndef _check_habitat(workspace_root: Path) -> DoctorCheck:\n    from .habitat import HABITAT_DEGRADED_FREE_GB, probe_habitat\n\n    facts = probe_habitat(workspace_root=workspace_root)\n    if facts.get(\"degraded\"):\n        return DoctorCheck(\n            \"habitat\", \"warn\",\n            f\"free_disk_below_{HABITAT_DEGRADED_FREE_GB:g}gb\", facts,\n        )\n    return DoctorCheck(\"habitat\", \"ok\", detail=facts)\n\n\ndef _requests_are_live(tools_dir: Path) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Producers have written: the store is past bootstrap, so a ledger the\n    producers feed cannot be legitimately absent.\"\"\"\n    requests = tools_dir / \"agent-invocations\" / \"requests.jsonl\"\n    return requests.is_file() and requests.stat().st_size > 0\n\n\ndef _plans_minted(tools_dir: Path) -> int:\n    \"\"\"How many plans the orchestrator has minted, from its own state ledger.\n\n    One ``PLAN_MINTED_PHASE`` transition per minted plan. Only rows that\n    carry ``FUNNEL_RECORDED_DETAIL`` count: the orchestrator stamps it after\n    writing the effectiveness ledger and immediately before emitting the\n    row, so for those rows a count above zero with no effectiveness rows is\n    a ledger that existed and is gone, or a write that failed and was\n    disclosed as a ``pressure_source_outcome_failed`` governance row. A row\n    without the stamp predates the ledger (the live store holds twenty from\n    2026-08 → 09-04, written when the writer sat on the converged path):\n    it says a plan was minted, not that a ledger row was due. Read by the\n    path the store keeps it at, not ``autonomy_state_path`` (which binds and\n    refreshes the tools root — a write the doctor must not make).\"\"\"\n    from .autonomy_state import FUNNEL_RECORDED_DETAIL, PLAN_MINTED_PHASE\n    from .ledger import load_declared_jsonl\n\n    path = tools_dir / \"autonomy_state.jsonl\"\n    if not path.is_file():\n        return 0\n    rows = load_declared_jsonl(path, expected_surface=\"autonomy_state\", verify=True)\n    return sum(\n        1 for row in rows\n        if row.get(\"phase\") == PLAN_MINTED_PHASE\n        and (row.get(\"details\") or {}).get(FUNNEL_RECORDED_DETAIL) is True\n    )\n\n\ndef _check_funnel(tools_dir: Path) -> DoctorCheck:\n    \"\"\"The funnel's counters, read where the store keeps them.\n\n    A STORE organ (B4, 2026-09-12): the effectiveness ledger is a\n    tools-root surface (``state_manifest`` kg_pressure_source_effectiveness)\n    and the state ledger it is judged against lives under the same root.\n    Reading ``<workspace>/aria-tools/...`` instead — as this organ did — is\n    right on a developer checkout and blind on the live lane, where\n    ARIA_TOOLS_DIR=<store>/tools and the workspace is the checkout: on\n    2026-09-12 the store held 807 requests, no effectiveness ledger, and\n    the organ said ok with sources=0.\n\n    The absence is judged against the orchestrator's OWN evidence of a\n    minted plan, not against the requests ledger: requests are minted by a\n    dozen producers (judge fan-out, expert review, cross-review) that run\n    with no plan in the funnel, so \"requests live\" does not imply \"a plan\n    was minted\" — a store with only those would have been a false fault.\n    A ``PLAN_MINTED_PHASE`` transition does imply it: the orchestrator\n    records the mint in the effectiveness ledger immediately before\n    emitting that transition, on every exit (invalid plan, blocked verdict,\n    converged), so the implication \"plan minted => effectiveness ledger\n    exists\" holds by construction, and a store of blocked-only cycles is\n    ok — or warn once the convergence stage has taken ten plans and\n    released none — with its counters in the detail, never a fault.\"\"\"\n    from .funnel_health import detect_funnel_stalls\n    from .knowledge_graph import effectiveness_ledger_path, rank_pressure_sources\n\n    rows = rank_pressure_sources(base_dir=tools_dir)\n    ledger = effectiveness_ledger_path(base_dir=tools_dir)\n    plans_minted = _plans_minted(tools_dir)\n    detail: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"sources\": len(rows),\n        \"effectiveness_ledger_present\": ledger.is_file() and ledger.stat().st_size > 0,\n        \"plans_minted\": plans_minted,\n        \"counters\": {\n            str(row.get(\"source_type\")): {\n                field: int(row.get(field, 0) or 0)\n                for field in (\"cycles_minted\", \"cycles_converged\", \"cycles_merged\", \"cycles_rejected\")\n            }\n            for row in rows\n        },\n    }\n    if not rows and plans_minted:\n        return DoctorCheck(\"funnel\", \"fail\", \"funnel_ledger_missing_with_minted_plans\", detail)\n    stalls = detect_funnel_stalls(rows)\n    if stalls:\n        detail[\"stalls\"] = [asdict(stall) for stall in stalls]\n        return DoctorCheck(\"funnel\", \"warn\", f\"funnel_stalled:{len(stalls)}\", detail)\n    return DoctorCheck(\"funnel\", \"ok\", detail=detail)\n\n\ndef _check_plan_ledger(tools_dir: Path) -> DoctorCheck:\n    \"\"\"The mechanism-2 finding of 2026-09-02: requests existed, the plan\n    ledger did not, and the drainer re-started the same plan every night.\n    A write-driving ledger that vanished while its producers kept writing\n    is a FAIL, not a bootstrap.\"\"\"\n    plans_dir = tools_dir / \"plans\"\n    plan_ledgers = sorted(plans_dir.glob(\"*.jsonl\")) if plans_dir.is_dir() else []\n    detail = {\n        \"requests_ledger_present\": (tools_dir / \"agent-invocations\" / \"requests.jsonl\").is_file(),\n        \"plan_ledgers\": [path.name for path in plan_ledgers],\n    }\n    if _requests_are_live(tools_dir) and not plan_ledgers:\n        return DoctorCheck(\"plan_ledger\", \"fail\", \"plan_ledger_missing_with_live_requests\", detail)\n    return DoctorCheck(\"plan_ledger\", \"ok\", detail=detail)\n\n\ndef _check_delivery(tools_dir: Path) -> DoctorCheck:\n    \"\"\"Plan 032 Faz 032d — the last mile has a reader. Duplicate PRs are a\n    fault (fail); an accepted result with no PR is a false success (warn —\n    the request may still be mid-flight); the SLO itself is detail.\"\"\"\n    from .delivery_closure import compute_delivery_closure\n\n    summary = compute_delivery_closure(base_dir=tools_dir).summary\n    if summary[\"duplicate_prs\"]:\n        return DoctorCheck(\"delivery_closure\", \"fail\", \"duplicate_prs\", summary)\n    if summary[\"false_success\"] or summary[\"unresolved_intents\"]:\n        return DoctorCheck(\"delivery_closure\", \"warn\", \"false_success_or_unresolved_intents\", summary)\n    if not summary[\"implementation_requests\"]:\n        return DoctorCheck(\"delivery_closure\", \"ok\", \"no_implementation_requests\", summary)\n    return DoctorCheck(\"delivery_closure\", \"ok\", \"\", summary)\n\n\ndef _check_queue(tools_dir: Path) -> DoctorCheck:\n    \"\"\"Plan 032 Faz 032e — queue depth by derived state + open HUMAN_REQUIRED.\"\"\"\n    from collections import Counter\n\n    from .agent_invocations import derive_request_states\n    from .human_required import list_human_required\n    from .mission import list_open_missions\n\n    states = Counter(derive_request_states(base_dir=tools_dir).values())\n    open_hr = list_human_required(base_dir=tools_dir)\n    missions = Counter(str(m.get(\"state\") or \"\") for m in list_open_missions(base_dir=tools_dir))\n    detail = {\"requests_by_state\": dict(sorted(states.items())), \"human_required_open\": len(open_hr),\n              \"missions_open_by_state\": dict(sorted(missions.items()))}\n    if open_hr:\n        return DoctorCheck(\"queue\", \"warn\", f\"human_required_open:{len(open_hr)}\", detail)\n    return DoctorCheck(\"queue\", \"ok\", \"\", detail)\n\n\ndef _check_control(tools_dir: Path) -> DoctorCheck:","numLines":260,"startLine":80,"totalLines":663}}}
